**REQUEST LIFECYCLE — Row B decides permission durably, and every state has one terminal treatment (repairing CODEX-VOCAB F1 and GPT56-VOCAB F1, both HIGH).** The derivation above assumed permission is *"evaluated before the attempt."* **The covenant nowhere imposed that ordering, and it is false in two ways** — a permission check can die before deciding, and a write's conformance cannot be judged before its payload is read. **Asserting the ordering is what failed; stating the machine is the repair.**
  **THE LIFECYCLE IS SPECIFIED IN `LIFECYCLE_GUARANTEE_SPEC.md` AND THIS TEXT IS DERIVED FROM IT — after the same object failed THREE consecutive rounds (V67 F1/F2, V68 F1/F2, V69 F1/F2), which is this lane's stop-patching threshold, invoked here as it was for the citation check.** The third failure was the most familiar shape in this corpus: **V69 deleted the `TRANSFER` state in one paragraph and this sentence still declared it** (GPT56-V69 F1, CODEX-V69 F1) — a deletion that did not delete. **The state machine now has ONE home, the spec, and this declaration quotes it:** `RECEIVED` → `PENDING-AUTHORISATION` → (writes only: `PENDING-SURFACE-CHECK`, because a field-constrained write's conformance cannot be known until Row B decodes the payload) → **one commit, refusal or touch** → (conveyance only: delivery, outside the custody claim). **There is no `TRANSFER` state.** The spec carries the full invariant table — SIX crash windows (W0–W5, W0/W1 re-cut at V91 when the arrival class split the pre-commit span) × its reader columns — and its guarantees G1–G6 and non-guarantees N1–N3 (GPT56-V91 F6: this unlabelled summary said five windows and G1–G5 after the spec had six and G6; the derivation checker's stated blind spot, exercised on the sentence beside the pin), **with G2 and G3 restated at V71 because their V70 wording contradicted refusal events outright — an event with no store effect violated "one touch per event", and G2 said nothing about its truth (GPT56-V70 F1, CODEX-V70 F1, the first finding scored against the spec itself): a refusal event truthfully records that NO effect occurred, and — in the V71 wording this recap preserves AS HISTORY — every event was exactly one touch's or one refusal's, never both, never neither; **the partition has been THREE-WAY since the arrival class (G3: ARRIVAL, one touch's, one refusal's) and this recap restored the two-way form as live prose one clause from the pin it summarizes (CODEX-V97 F2 — the derivation checker's unlabelled-prose blind spot, exercised again)**; **a conflict between this draft and the spec is a defect in the draft.**
  **THE GUARANTEE — QUOTED FROM THE SPEC BY DIGEST, NOT RESTATED (GPT56-V71 F1, CODEX-V71 F1).** V71 amended the spec's G2/G3 and left this block carrying the V70-broken wording — the exact one-round death the derivation claim was warned about, arrived in mirror form: the warning said do not fix the draft and leave the spec behind; V71 fixed the spec and left the draft behind. **The derivation is now a CHECK, not a claim**: `tools/lifecycle_derivation_check.py` fails when the pin below goes stale or any quoted invariant body diverges from the spec's bytes — a spec edit breaks the pin until the draft re-pins, which forces the re-derivation instead of permitting the drift. **This class of finding is now impossible rather than unlikely, which is the difference between a predicate and a name.**
  lifecycle-spec: sha256 `e087d932673a81b029e2ff42adb0394394f708230172fdde5ce2d4b464efa06a`
  **G1 — **No unlogged touch**: no bytes leave or land in a sealed store without a committed event**
  **G2 — **No false event**: a touch event's outcome field is true of the store effect it records; **a refusal event truthfully records that a request was refused and NO store effect occurred — AND its reason token is true of the refusal: a request with no completed permission verdict may carry only `REFUSED-UNCLASSIFIED`, and any specific code asserts its condition was actually established** (GPT56-V71 F2: without this, a false specific code bypasses the catch-all enumeration entirely) — **and an ARRIVAL event is under this invariant too: it truthfully records RECEIPT — its identifying facts are those of one real request, appended before any processing of that request (extended over the arrival class, GPT56-V88 F1)****
  **WHAT CAN CHECK THAT — refined at V79 because both halves of the V78 split were too coarse (GPT56-V78 F4: immutable chain history can make a false `OBJECT-ABSENT` audit-provable, so availability is not uniformly testimony; CODEX-V78 F5: a normative truth condition with no CONSUMING verifier is prose). Per code: the five authorisation codes are RECOMPUTABLE from the chain and pinned artifacts; **`REFUSED-SCHEMA-NONCONFORMING` is NOT — the chain is payload-free by design, so nobody can recompute what the refused payload contained: it is TESTIMONY, and V80's table put it on the wrong side (GPT56-V80 F2)**; `REFUSED-OBJECT-ABSENT` is **contradiction-surfacing from history** — a prior committed touch proves PAST presence, not present absence's falsity (GPT56-V79 F3, CODEX-V79 F3, verbatim-convergent); but the stores are append-only and mutation is forbidden, so presence is monotone, and **history + a later ABSENT proves the DISJUNCTION: either the code is false or a forbidden removal occurred — both audit-findings, neither silent, and the audit surfaces the contradiction rather than adjudicating it. The join is on (STORE identity, brickid, objid) — store identity derived from the event's row and stated surface, **and the derivation is per OBJECT CLASS, stated after GPT56-V104 F4 found the classes conflated: a row whose surface spans stores (Row I reads labels in the committee store AND instrument outputs in the main store) derives the store from the OPERATION's object class, not the row alone — label-class objects to the committee store, measurement-class to the main store — so the history join never pairs across stores for a multi-store row** — because brickid/objid alone can pair touches of different objects in different stores (CODEX-V80 F4)**; `REFUSED-OBJECT-UNREADABLE`, `-INCOMPLETE` and `-INTEGRITY-MISMATCH` assert store states no later reader can replay and are testimony; `REFUSED-UNCLASSIFIED` is checked by the enumeration machinery. **The CONSUMER is named: the §11 audit pass — the enumeration verifier's sibling clause — recomputes every recomputable condition and the history-falsifiable one for EVERY specific refusal in the chain, at the same five gates.** The original statement (GPT56-V77 F5): codes whose conditions are RECOMPUTABLE from the chain and pinned artifacts — row surfaces, phase, lock state, precondition existence — are audit-checkable post hoc, and the auditor recomputes them; the availability codes assert store states no later reader can replay, and their truth is TESTIMONY under Row B's signature, named as such. A false specific code in the recomputable class is detectable; in the testimony class it is attributable. Neither is silent**
  **G3 — **One TOUCH event per touch** — and **the event classes PARTITION: every committed event is exactly one of an ARRIVAL event (§1c), one touch's event, or one refusal's event — no event is two of these and no event is none** (re-derived over the arrival class — GPT56-V88 F1: the pre-arrival form said every event is a touch's or a refusal's and no event is neither, which forbade the class the same sitting authorised; V70's lesson — an event partition must name ALL its classes, GPT56-V70 F1, CODEX-V70 F1 — applied to three)**
  **G4 — **No double decision**: one request never yields two DECISION events — a touch commit or a refusal commit — and, since the WRITE-AHEAD ARRIVAL RECEIPT was authorised as its own event class (principal ruling, 2026-08-30 10:46), a completed request carries exactly ONE arrival event and ONE decision event; this invariant counts decisions only. The pre-arrival form said two EVENTS bare, which condemned the arrival receipt the same sitting authorised — swept as a set with GPT56/CODEX-V87 F1.**
  **G5 — **Render = touch.** Every render is its own touch with its own committed event. **Row G's *"any unlogged view"* void clause is a consumer of G5, not an exception to N1**
  **And what it does NOT guarantee, with equal weight:**
  **N1 — **Delivery is outside the custody claim** — the event records the store effect, never the requester's receipt or the human's perception**
  **N2 — **RETIRED BY RULING (2026-08-30 10:46): the WRITE-AHEAD ARRIVAL RECEIPT makes every real request durably visible** — arrival is logged BEFORE any processing, as a second event class the principal explicitly authorised, so no request can vanish and the lifecycle promise becomes true instead of narrowed. Kept in the table as the record of what was a non-guarantee for eleven revisions**
  **N3 — **The log can over-report delivery, never under-report a touch**
  **And the TERMINATION properties, same discipline (§3c, re-derived in one pass after V102 — the checker binds these labels too):**
  **T1 — **Admission CLOSES at DRAIN-START**: when any TERMINATED-family condition fires, its receipt is made durable FIRST — the detecting instrument writes it to the receipt store before anything else, so the drain-start never references an absent receipt (CODEX-V103 F3), and a recovery finding a standing receipt with no drain-start appends the drain-start itself — then Row B appends the DRAIN-START record — checkpoint-family, clock-bearing, closed schema `(kind, receipt_digest, boot_epoch, monotonic_reading)` (GPT56-V106 F2, CODEX-V106 F3: the single-surface move deleted the only place this tuple lived; the surface now carries it) — **between requests: any fully decoded frame in Row B's hands completes its arrival commit first (one bounded append by the same serialised writer — GPT56-V103 F2: a decoded unit must not lose the race to the closure its own receipt transition promised), while frames not yet fully decoded are wire residue**; after drain-start the receipt transition is CLOSED — no frame received, no arrival appended, the no-vanish guarantee scoped to requests received before it. **AT MOST ONE drain-start per chain (GPT56-V103 F3): a TERMINATED condition firing during DRAIN-OPEN appends its receipt to the store AND a RECEIPT-NOTE record to the chain — checkpoint-family, `(kind, receipt_digest, boot_epoch, monotonic_reading)` — because THE LOG IS THE AUTHORITY AND THE STORE IS ITS STAGING, NEVER A PEER — refined at V106 after both seats caught the write-ORDER reading contradicting T1's own receipt-durable-first sequence (GPT56/CODEX-V105 F1): the store write may PRECEDE in time, but a fact becomes OPERATIVE only at its log record — a store receipt without its log record (drain-start or receipt-note) is an ORPHAN, and THE GATE RULE IS ABSOLUTE — at every verifier gate pass, any orphan refuses, no staging window (GPT56/CODEX-V107 F2 killed `B_stage`: a chain-position bound never expires on a quiet chain and could ride through every gate, and the window contradicted the regardless-refusal this row already stated; the only staging that exists is Row B's own ordering obligation — the drain-start is the receipt's NEXT chain append under the precondition chain, fixture-tested — and the pass uses the DOUBLE-READ DISCIPLINE (GPT56-V108 F3, CODEX-V108 F2: the mandated receipt-first sequence has a real interval — store write landed, Row B's next append not yet — and an unsynchronised gate would refuse the very ordering T1 requires): the verifier reads store and chain head, then reads both again; and the verdict is MOTION-FREE per §0b (the coordinator's rule after V109; both seats broke the head-advanced precondition with a receipt written just before Row B dies — motionless, passing forever): the orphan predicate is SEQUENCE-BOUNDED — neither motion-forever nor instant condemnation (CODEX-V111 F3 broke the absolute form: it condemned the mandated receipt-first interval itself, since a pass's boundary could interpose between store write and log append; the coordinator's bound): the receipt-first sequence is an INDIVISIBLE ROW-B UNIT — the store write and its log append (drain-start or receipt-note) are ONE serialised act, no other append may interleave, and Row B does not begin a verification pass inside a unit — a BS-2k writer obligation with its own stall fixture, testimony-plus-fixture like every writer obligation; therefore a receipt is an ORPHAN exactly when the log holds ANY append later than its store write without its record — the mandated interval spans zero appends, so the first later append (a verification boundary included) either IS the receipt's record or convicts the unit, and both readings refuse: one as orphan, one as unit-violation malformed history; no conforming staged receipt is condemnable because its record precedes every later append; DISCLOSURE RUNS THE RECONCILIATION AS A MANDATORY FINAL PASS with no precondition of any kind; authority, not chronology, is what the store never has; the drain already runs, the terminal checkpoint binds the FIRST receipt, and the gates refuse on any receipt-note or store receipt regardless****
  **T2 — **THE DRAIN SET IS DEFINED ONCE, AT DRAIN-START, FROM THE LOG (the coordinator's rule after V103): every arrival with chain position at or before the DRAIN-START event and no terminal — deadline-expired ones INCLUDED (GPT56-V103 F1, CODEX-V103 F1: draining only unexpired PENDING could checkpoint over an overdue orphan), and the in-hand frames T1 commits ahead of the drain-start (GPT56-V103 F2) — is IN the drain obligation; nothing joins later and nothing leaves.** DRAIN-OPEN is a chain state; recovery in it opens a DRAINING EPOCH, lawful and constrained: it gives each drain-set member its FORCED TERMINAL under `REFUSED-LOCK-OR-CEREMONY-STATE` — truthful for every member, expired or not: the ceremony state does not permit completion (the coordinator sketched naming a new code for the forced terminal; the existing one says the same true thing and the ruled eleven stay untouched) — in arrival order, idempotently (already-terminal members skip), admitting nothing, and appends the terminal checkpoint when every member's decision event is committed — individually, or inside the checkpoint commit itself (the third surviving drained-or-listed clause, swept — GPT56-V108 F2); **EVERY drain member gets a FIRST-CLASS DECISION EVENT, cap-exhausted ones included — the listing is a PROJECTION, never a mechanism (the coordinator's store-is-a-projection principle applied once more; GPT56/CODEX-V107 F1 killed terminal-by-listing: a member terminalized by appearing in a list occupied no lifecycle slot and broke exactly-one-decision): a member whose individual refusal commit exhausts `A_max` aborts receives its refusal event INSIDE THE TERMINAL CHECKPOINT'S OWN ATOMIC COMMIT — one commit, many writes, the issuance-commit precedent — so every member has exactly one ordinary refusal event under the ordinary schema, G4 holds with no third form, the crash-window cells apply normally, and `failed_members` merely ENUMERATES the members whose events rode the checkpoint commit (a projection of log events carried in the same commit; if the checkpoint commit itself exhausts `A_max`, that is the platform failing, already stated); after the checkpoint, a zero-terminal arrival is malformed history — no exceptions, the drained-or-listed disjunction is gone because listing no longer substitutes for an event.** **THE DRAIN-SCOPED ORDERING LAW (GPT56-V108 F1, CODEX-V108 F1: deferred in-commit events land after later members' individual commits, inverting ordinary FIFO): ordinary FIFO ends at drain-start; within the drain, EVERY ATTEMPT EMITS ITS OWN LOG RECORD AT ATTEMPT-START, UNCONDITIONALLY (the coordinator's fix over V110's testimony demotion — order derives from bytes for every future deferral shape, per §0b): the ATTEMPT-START record — checkpoint-family, clock-bearing, closed schema `(kind, member_position, boot_epoch, monotonic_reading)`; a checkpoint-family record, not a new EVENT class, because the log's ruled event classes are untouched and the clock family already appends — lands before each attempt, aborted or not, so ATTEMPT ORDER IS CHAIN FACT: attempt-start records must be arrival-ordered, each member's decision (individual or in-commit) must follow its own last attempt-start, and the deferred in-commit events stay arrival-ordered among themselves. **AND EVERY START IS CLOSED — THE COUNTED THING IS A PAIR, NEVER A BARE START (GPT56-V111 F8, CODEX-V111 F4: a start record narrates; A_max bare records could be minted with zero attempts and the recount would bless them; the coordinator's rule — pair every start with a mandatory outcome-or-abort event and count PAIRS): the ATTEMPT-CLOSE record — checkpoint-family, closed schema `(kind, member_position, close_class, boot_epoch, monotonic_reading)` with `close_class` from the closed two-token set {`ABORTED`, `ABORTED-BY-RESTART`}; a SUCCESSFUL attempt needs no close record because the member's own decision event IS its close — and the ALTERNATION LAW, chain-checkable: per member, in chain order, attempt records strictly alternate start → close-or-decision → start; at most ONE open start exists per member and only as that member's latest attempt record; a second start over an open start is malformed history. A crash leaves a dangling start, and the opening-linked epochs make it visible: the next epoch's FIRST drain act for that member must append `ABORTED-BY-RESTART` before any new start — the aborted-attempt hole closes one record later, not never. EXHAUSTION IS A PAIR COUNT: a member deferred into the terminal checkpoint must show ≥ `A_max` CLOSED aborted pairs (each start joined to its `ABORTED`/`ABORTED-BY-RESTART`), the verifier counts pairs and refuses bare starts toward the count. THE RESIDUE, named rather than laundered: a pair proves the writer CLAIMED an attempt, bracketed in clock-bearing chain fact — it does not prove execution, because an aborted attempt leaves no other bytes BY DEFINITION; execution is the drain loop's implementation obligation, fixture-tested, SAID to be testimony-plus-fixture — what the pair law removes is the free-standing count, so forging exhaustion now costs 2·A_max alternating records under the enumerator key inside the drain's own budgeted window, inside the named trust boundary, not one loop of appends.**** **Drain refusals satisfy their own DRAIN-SCOPED acceptance predicate, GROUNDED IN LOG FACTS (CODEX-V104 F2 called the V104 form circular; GPT56-V104 F6 asked for the computable form): a refusal is a valid drain terminal iff (i) its target is a DRAIN-SET MEMBER — membership fixed at the drain-start event, a log fact — and (ii) its own chain position lies in the open interval (drain-start position, terminal-checkpoint position) — a CHAIN-POSITION interval, epoch-independent, computable across any number of recovery epochs. **AND THE ABORT LOOP IS CAPPED (GPT56-V104 F5, CODEX-V104 F2's second half): a member whose forced refusal aborts `A_max` times (a BS-2k constant) is SKIPPED and listed in the terminal checkpoint's `failed_members` field — a commit that cannot land after `A_max` bounded attempts is the platform failing, §3b's family, and the checkpoint says so per member rather than looping forever or lying that the drain completed clean.** THE PRECONDITION CHAIN IS THE ONLY ORDERING: receipt durable in the store → drain-start binding its digest → per-member terminals under DRAIN-OPEN → terminal checkpoint requiring EVERY MEMBER'S DECISION EVENT COMMITTED — individually during the drain, or inside the checkpoint commit for the cap-exhausted (GPT56-V108 F2, CODEX-V108 F1: the drained-or-listed wording survived in three clauses one round after listing stopped being a terminal form; the disjunction is gone because both branches are now the same thing — an event) — each step's precondition is the prior step's durable event, and no other ordering exists (CODEX-V103 F3)**; ordinary admission never resumes**
  **T3 — **The TERMINAL CHECKPOINT is a first-class artifact and THE AUTHENTICATED LOG IS THE ROOT**: closed non-χ schema `(kind, drain_start_position, receipt_digest, chain_head_position, chain_head_digest, boot_epoch, monotonic_reading, failed_members)` — synced with the V105 field the draft carried alone (GPT56-V105 F3, CODEX-V105 F3: seven- and eight-field forms coexisted, and L08's name-set grouping is blind to pure ADDITION — supersets are legitimate between records, so the repair is single-sourcing, not another heuristic) — every field value-domained, the record ON the exhaustive non-χ list, IN the domain-kind set, and UNDER the clock pass like every clock-bearing record; its digest is what the terminal ceremony signs AFTER recomputation; **THE TERMINAL CHECKPOINT IS THE EXPORT TRIGGER for a terminated run — the SUCCESSOR EXPORT is part of the drain-close artifact set, emitted in the same serialised step, canonical and complete, never a reward for finishing well (the coordinator on ruling 6 after V109: a run that dies badly must hand off what it learned — that was the whole point); a clean run's export is emitted INSIDE THE SAME ATOMIC COMMIT as the disclosure pass record at the mandatory reconciliation pass — never before it, so a termination during reconciliation pre-commit finds NO clean export and emits the terminated form alone, and EXACTLY ONE export exists per run (GPT56-V113 F5: an early clean export could survive a mid-reconciliation termination as a stale authenticated sibling; a history carrying two successor-facing exports is malformed outright, and post-run termination is outside every custody claim by the terminal-head boundary already stated); **and a run terminated BEFORE BS-L exports the PRE-LOCK FORM — kind `successor-export-prelock` — (GPT56-V112 F9: no sealed set exists before the partition, and an unconstructible field is not a degraded one — the export degrades BY RULE, absent-by-form, never by sentinel): `(kind, terminal_enumeration_digest, terminal_head, freeze_signature_digest, flagged_keys)` — `terminal_enumeration_digest` the count-prefixed, chain-position-sorted canonical entry bodies AS OF THE DRAIN CUT under the enumerator keypair; there is no sealed/continuation split before the partition exists, SAID rather than faked, and the trust boundary is likewise said: pre-lock entries rest on the provisioned key alone between P0 and the never-reached P6, exactly the freeze-to-lock window §3b already names; the verifier refuses a post-lock run emitting the pre-lock form and a pre-lock run emitting the sealed form**; and the artifact set names the log itself as the trust root, because claiming any other root would name a thing that is not there (GPT56-V102 F6, CODEX-V102 F3, and both F1s' root complaint)**
  **THE CONSTRUCTION, derived from the guarantee.** A **TOUCH COMMIT** is one atomic commit in the BS-2k transactional domain comprising: the **store effect** (bytes leaving the store into Row B's committed buffer, or a validated write landing in the store), the **one event carrying that effect's true outcome**, and **Row B's identifier binding**. A **REFUSAL COMMIT** is the same commit with no store effect — an event and a binding. **DELIVERY is a separate act after the touch commit, executed from Row B's committed buffer** — and the retry allowance is **CONVEYANCE-ONLY, stated where the allowance lives because both seats found the unscoped sentence re-authorising what G5 forbids (GPT56-V73 F1, CODEX-V73 F7)**: re-sending a **conveyance** buffer to the same row is the same conveyance of the same authorised touch, not a second touch and never a second event; **a RENDER is never re-sent — every re-display is a new touch commit under G5, and a render buffer lives from its commit until its VIEW SESSION ends, then is destroyed** (CODEX-V74 F2). **And a committed render event asserts CONVEYANCE TO THE INTERFACE — bytes left the store — never that a human saw a frame (GPT56-V80 F3: a crash before first frame leaves a committed event and no view, which is a TRUE event about a conveyance whose session never opened; the no-session buffer path already destroys it at request end, and Row G's unlogged-view clause is untouched because no view occurred).** **And the sealed interface renders exclusively from Row B conveyances and holds NO redisplayable surface beyond the live session** (GPT56-V74 F3: a compositor restore after occlusion would create G6's new view with no G5 commit; occlude-and-restore must produce a second committed render event or no image — a BS-2k interface requirement with fixtures).
  **THE CORNERS NOW ENUMERATE, which is what stating the guarantee buys:**
  — **Death before any commit**: no store effect, no DECISION event, no binding, no bytes moved — and, since the arrival ruling, ONE committed event all the same: the ARRIVAL appended at receipt, which shows the request existed and hands recovery its work item. G1 and G4 hold with zero decisions; G2 holds of the arrival's own truth; the corner is visible, not vacuous (re-derived from §1c — GPT56-V88 F3, CODEX-V88 F2: the pre-arrival corner said "no event" and a corrective tail contradicted it in the same sentence).
  — **Death at the commit**: it is atomic; there is no partial state to enumerate.
  — **Death after commit, before or during delivery**: the event is TRUE — the store effect happened — and the requester may have nothing. **That is N1, accepted, and it is the only over-report this design permits.** Delivery retries from the committed buffer without a new event — **CONVEYANCE ONLY: a render is never re-sent, every re-display is a new touch commit under G5 (GPT56-V93 F8: this corner's unscoped sentence re-authorised what §5 forbids, the V73 finding's shape one corner over).**
  — **Bytes escaping an aborted commit**: impossible by construction — **the requester receives only from the committed buffer, and an aborted commit never produces one.**
  — **A write dying in validation**: the payload sits in Row B's pre-commit staging; no store effect occurred; the request stands as an ARRIVAL with no decision — PENDING until its deadline or a recovery pass drives it to the refusal commit (re-derived — GPT56-V88 F3, CODEX-V88 F2).
  — **A change of terminal fact after the event**: **cannot exist.** The terminal fact IS the store effect, committed with its event in the same transaction. V68's `TRANSFER` state — a post-verdict period in which the outcome could still change — **is deleted, and its death rule with it**: there is no state after the commit whose failure alters anything the log claims (GPT56-V68 F2, CODEX-V68 F2).
  **What remains of the state machine:** `RECEIVED` → `PENDING-AUTHORISATION` → (writes: `PENDING-SURFACE-CHECK`) → **one commit, refusal or touch** → (reads: delivery from the committed buffer, outside the custody claim). **The contract is a requirement on the BS-2k design artifacts (§7 Row A) and §11 carries its implementation item; until BS-2k is filled with a design meeting it, this lifecycle is specified and not yet operative.**
  **WHAT A REQUEST IS, because "never re-decided" is unenforceable without it (CODEX-V66 F2, HIGH — re-derived over the arrival class, GPT56-V88 F3: the pre-arrival sentence said the identifier was "not written to the access log", true only while writing one was unauthorised).** A request has TWO identities with different homes: Row B's **internal identifier**, assigned on receipt and held in Row B's own recovery state — private, never logged; and the request's **logged identity, which is its ARRIVAL event** — `request_key` = that event's chain position, on chain by the ruling that authorised the class. The binding→key map joins the two. **A legal retry is a NEW request with a NEW identifier and its OWN arrival, and produces its OWN decision event**: the covenant requires *"exactly one event per touch"*, and a retry is a second touch. **What is forbidden is one request producing two DECISION events (G4)**, and identity — now logged, not merely internal — is what makes that statement testable by the verifier rather than by trust in Row B's recovery state. **Repeated touches of the same object are expected and are not redecision.**
  **ONE DECISION PER REQUEST, AND ROW B OWNS RECOVERY — and the recovery rule is the EXISTENCE half of exactly-one (GPT56-V88 F4, CODEX-V88 F3).** Row B is a **single serialised writer** with **at most one decision in flight per request**. On recovery Row B consults its **committed bindings**, which the touch commit made atomic with the events, AGAINST the logged arrivals: a request **with a binding is never re-decided**; a request **with an arrival and no binding is a PENDING request, not a phantom** — recovery resumes it from the logged arrival (never appending a second arrival, per §1c) and **drives it to its one decision**: through the normal decision path while its §3b deadline stands, and to the deadline refusal commit the moment it does not. §3b already makes past-deadline-and-live a refusal and bounds the commit itself; recovery is the actor that applies it to arrivals found open, which is what makes "every request reaches a terminal" a mechanism instead of a hope. **A worker may not consult the chain instead of the bindings — recomputing custody from the file being recovered is the hash-chain-launders-tampering shape; the arrival is the work item, the binding is the decision record, and they answer different questions.** **A worker that has lost its lease may not append** — the lease is Row B's, and losing it means the work is abandoned, not resumed — **and an abandoned request's arrival still stands, so abandonment is visible and the deadline still closes it.**
  **WHAT THIS DID NOT FIX IS NOW FIXED BY RULING (2026-08-30 10:46): the WRITE-AHEAD ARRIVAL RECEIPT is the second event class, and changing what the log records is exactly what the principal authorised.** Arrival is durably logged before any processing, so a request in flight when Row B died has its arrival event — eleven revisions of the invisible pre-verdict death end as a logged arrival with no terminal event, a state recovery and the auditor can SEE and the deadline machinery closes. Spec §1c carries the class; N2 is retired in the table as history. **What the rules above achieve:** no request is both undecided and delivered, none is decided twice, and none stays undecided past its deadline. **What remains — the residue re-drawn at its true boundary (GPT56-V88 F3, CODEX-V88 F2: the old sentence kept the pre-arrival residue, calling decide-and-append death indistinguishable from non-arrival — the committed arrival is precisely what distinguishes them):** only the PRE-ARRIVAL WIRE is dark — **and its boundary is DEFINED, not chosen after a crash (GPT56-V89 F2, CODEX-V89 F5): §1c's RECEIPT TRANSITION — a request exists exactly when its complete framed unit is read and decoded AND its arrival's framed unit is durably committed; frame decode is receipt, not processing; a truncated or malformed frame is by definition not a request, and a crash mid-arrival-append fails digest closure and is no arrival.** A request that dies before its arrival append never entered the log's world, and no rule here can speak of it; everything after the arrival append is visible, pending, and closed by deadline or decision — WITHIN WHAT THE PLATFORM SERVES, §3b's own quantifier (GPT56-V95 F6: this corner claimed totality without the storage-hang carve-out its source states; a hung fsync leaves an overdue arrival unterminated and the run ends by operator observation), and AS OF THE ANCHORS, §3b's window residue (GPT56-V95 F1). **That is the whole residue and it is stated as one.**
  **TERMINAL TREATMENT, fixed per state — and the fix distinguishes WHO died, which V68 did not.** A request whose **processing fails while Row B survives** — a worker timeout, a deadlock, a lost verifier — receives a **refusal commit carrying `REFUSED-UNCLASSIFIED`**: Row B is alive to append it, and the request ends logged. **And "fails" cannot be outwaited (CODEX-V80 F5): every request carries a DEADLINE — now specified in the spec's §3b as lifecycle semantics with its single home there (GPT56-V81 F2, CODEX-V81 F4: V81 put the deadline in this draft and not in the pinned spec, violating the single-home rule with the very repair that extended the lifecycle): a BS-2k design constant on the MONOTONIC clock, fixed at receipt, never reset — after which a live pending request IS a failure and receives that refusal commit.** A request in flight **when Row B itself dies before any commit** now has its ARRIVAL event (the ruling's second event class): **visible as an arrival with no terminal, which the deadline machinery closes on recovery** — the old N2 invisibility is retired, and only the pre-arrival wire remains dark. **There is no `TRANSFER` death rule because there is no `TRANSFER` state**: after the commit the terminal fact is already true and logged, and a delivery failure changes nothing the log claims. **`REFUSED-UNCLASSIFIED` is where undecided permission lands, and the continuous enumeration above — verified at `BS-L` issuance and again at the opening — is what keeps that visible instead of routine.**
  **A tension recorded rather than resolved:** a verifier timeout is **foreseeable**, and routing a foreseeable class into a category defined as a defect-to-be-enumerated is in tension with itself. **The clean answer is probably a named pre-decision state rather than a catch-all entry** — but that is a code, and adding one is close to the instruction that the set not be extended by hand, so it is flagged for the principal and not taken here.
  **What the log records changed exactly twice, each by ruling, and this sentence is the ledger of that (GPT56-V88 F3, CODEX-V88 F2: its predecessor said "nothing above changes what the access log records", written before the second ruling and left standing after it):** the refusal-reason field (2026-08-29) and the ARRIVAL event class (2026-08-30 10:46). No third change is made and no second custody surface is created — entries, explanations and the binding→key map live in checkpoint materials, not the log. The lifecycle remains a constraint on **when Row B decides and logs**, and the two authorised changes are the only changes to **what it writes**.

**THE ACCESS SCHEDULE IS PRECOMMITTED AND χ-BLIND (principal ruling, 2026-08-29 22:49, on converging findings from both seats).** **§2.7(3) constrains the EXCLUSION PREDICATES. It does not constrain the ACCESS SCHEDULE, and the χ-blindness this covenant relied on was inherited from it — an inheritance that does not reach.** GPT56 states the gap exactly: **the universe of eligible objects is χ-blind; the set and sequence actually requested are not.** CODEX states the consequence: ***a sign-blind exclusion rule is not a substitute for a sign-blind access schedule.***
  **What was found, and it was found against this table rather than against a general theory.** A conforming Row D runner may read object 1, compute its χ-derived output inside the sealed store, and choose object 2 over object 3 as its next read on the strength of that output; if the chosen cutout is absent or unreadable, **the access log records the refused object's identity, and that identity reveals which χ-dependent branch was taken.** The same shape reaches Row G with a human: view an allocated cutout, let the visual impression decide which allocated object to request next. **Row G required no fixed traversal, no fixed order and no precommitted schedule, and Row D's stated surface imposed none.**
  **THE RULE, and it FORBIDS rather than observes.** For **every row that may read a χ-bearing object**, the **object set and its complete traversal — order, multiplicity, retries and stopping — are PRECOMMITTED and χ-BLIND**, and **Row B refuses any request that is not the next unserved position in the applicable traversal.** **This is a prohibition on the covenant, not a description of current practice.** Nothing built today is adaptive — the cutout runner has no retry, no re-request and no reordering, and Row G's sequence is constructed by the hand-check design rather than by the checker — **but the covenant PERMITTED adaptivity, and permission is what leaks.** A clause that merely recorded that nobody currently exercises it would leave the channel open to the first implementation that did.
  **The traversal is DERIVED from objects already pinned; it is not a new artifact and creates no new slot.** For Row D it is the accepted sample in canonical `(brickid, objid)` order. For Row G it is the sealed allocation in the order the hand-check presentation design fixes — **that design already builds the sequence**, because its interleaved blind synthetics and its mirrored re-presentations at randomised later positions cannot survive a subject who chooses what to see next. **Multiplicity is part of the traversal**: a position may appear more than once, and it appears where the design puts it, not where a reader asks for it. **Re-viewing the CURRENT object is logged in CUSTODY terms by G5, and its MULTIPLICITY IS FIXED BY THE INTERFACE, because "unrestricted in schedule terms" contradicted the traversal rule three sentences up (GPT56-V91 F3: the rule freezes order, multiplicity, retries and stopping for every χ-bearing reader, and an unrestricted re-view count is a content-driven multiplicity exported into the non-χ log — one render request per interesting image, several per hard one, readable by any chain consumer): **the interface's multiplicity rule now lives in the spec (§5) and this text derives from it: EXACTLY `R_max` committed render events per allocated object per member — interruptions consume replays, and the padding is PER-OBJECT, issued when the member proceeds AWAY from an object, and the chain pattern is Row-H-TRUE: every object shows exactly `R_max` contiguous render commits and NO per-object label event — Row H persists the ONE label set as its row states — with inter-object PACE the spec's named leftover (GPT56-V95 F4, CODEX-V95 F1: this sentence kept the renders-then-label-write pattern and the no-timing-channel claim for one revision after the spec retired both) (CODEX-V93 F4 broke the V93 end-clustered form by timing; GPT56-V93 F5 and CODEX-V93 F3 broke "session-set end", which the BS-2k labeling deadline now backstops; GPT56-V93 F6 lineage, RE-RULED 2026-08-30 20:54 KST — ABSTAIN (the principal, verbatim "abstain", `EXHAUSTION_ABSTAIN_RULING_20260830.md`): a replay-exhausted object takes an ABSTAIN label — one more value in Row H's sealed label vocabulary, no new event class, the chain pattern unchanged: exactly `R_max` render commits and no per-object label event — and the run CONTINUES; the superseded rule said exhaustion HALTS the run pre-BS-8f, and that halt survives for everything else it covered; sample completeness re-reads as EVERY-OBJECT-LABELED with ABSTAIN a label — an abstained object is labeled but contributes no handedness call and drops from the analysis sample, honestly; abstention is caused by interruption count, procedural and fixed-budget, handedness-blind by the padding argument) (V92's single-pass form was broken by both seats against §5's own occlude-and-restore requirement — GPT56-V92 F4, CODEX-V92 F2 — an interrupted member had no route to a terminal label; padding keeps the constant count AND the replay route).** The earlier V69 collision (schedule vs custody) stays dissolved by G5 exactly as before. **The decisions file carries the updated flag: the ergonomics cost is now bounded replays rather than none, plus log inflation of (R_max − consumed) padding events per object.** A re-render is not a request for a *different* object, so it never violates the traversal — **and every render is its own touch with its own committed event**, which is what Row G's *"any unlogged view"* void clause always required: V69's delivery carve-out was correct for machine conveyance and **over-broad in covering renders**. **G6 — **A view is the display session of one render commit**: it ends at the first of position advance, interface clear, or any interruption of continuous display — visibility loss, blanking, occlusion, navigation away; duration alone does not multiply views, nothing displayed after an interruption is the same view, and commit↔session ownership is one-to-one — each render commit opens at most one session and every session is opened by exactly one commit**
  (CODEX-V72 F3 found the V72 sentence here paraphrasing the boundary in unlabelled prose — it named only two session-enders after the spec gained the interruption clause — which is exactly the checker's stated blind spot; the sentence is now a LABELLED quote the hardened checker binds, and the checker itself is wired into the lint as blocking, per GPT56-V72 F1). Within a session, dwell and magnification of the already-rendered frame move no store bytes and are the same logged view. **And the committed buffer is governed, not a χ-bearing holding surface**: renders get **no buffer reuse** — each re-render re-conveys under its own commit — and a conveyance buffer is destroyed on delivery completion or request end, its bounds a **BS-2k design requirement with fixtures**. **Retries are a fixed per-position attempt limit frozen in this preregistration**, so a transient storage failure is retried the same number of times everywhere and the count reveals nothing; the limit's value is a preregistered parameter fixed at freeze. **Stopping is the traversal being exhausted**; there is no early stop, and `require_complete_sample()`'s refusal of a partial run already says why.
  **THE ONE EXEMPTION — `flag → discard → replace`, AND IT IS RECORDED AS A FINDING, NOT AS A PERMISSION (principal ruling, 2026-08-29 22:49).** HC-1H's predeclared escape hatch lets the checker flag items as **suspected-identifiable** during a session, before key opening; those items are discarded and fresh draws from the same stratum and category are substituted. **That makes the realised set depend on an in-session, content-derived judgement, and it is exempt from the rule above.** **It stays exactly as HC-1H writes it, unmodified.**
  **WHY it does not leak, stated because an unexplained exemption invites a later editor to widen it or delete it, and both would be wrong for reasons nobody could reconstruct.** The principal's finding: **recognising a specific galaxy and judging its handedness are separate judgements, and nothing about handedness is visible in the image.** The flag responds to **identity** cues — *this looks synthetic*, *I have seen this before* — and **no instrument signs are visible to the checker at any point**. **The exemption rests on that finding, from the person who designed the procedure, and not on convenience.**
  **THE DEPENDENCY, written so it can be CHECKED rather than assumed — and written this way because the last dependency of this kind was assumed and the seats found it unpaid.** The exemption holds **only while flagging is uncorrelated with handedness**. **If a different interface, visible orientation cues, or a checker who also sees instrument output ever makes flagging handedness-sensitive, the exemption FAILS and the discard record becomes a χ channel.** **This is a condition to be preserved, not a fact that stays true by itself.**
  **How it is checked:** the exemption is **bound to the pinned digest of the Row G interface specification**, exactly as Row L's signature exemption is bound to canonical body digests rather than to a name. **Any change to that interface — to what the checker can see, to how items are displayed, or to what else the checker holds — changes the digest and LAPSES THE EXEMPTION**, and the hand check may not proceed under it until the finding is re-established by the principal. **An exemption bound to a name would widen with the name; bound to a digest it cannot widen silently.**

(ii-b) **the ARRIVAL event under its own authenticated closed schema — `(kind=ARRIVAL, timestamp, boot_epoch, monotonic_reading, row, operation, object identity, request_key, frame_sequence, request_digest, running chain digest)`** — identities, the same CLOSED operation set as item (ii), an ISO-8601 timestamp (human-facing — NEVER the deadline basis), **the authenticated clock pair `(boot_epoch, monotonic_reading)` — Row B's own monotonic reading at append, the durable start §3b's deadline is measured from (GPT56-V89 F1, CODEX-V89 F2); `boot_epoch` a decimal integer in [0, 10^6] — the BS-2k restart counter, a sequence number owing nothing to any clock — and `monotonic_reading` a decimal integer nanosecond count in [0, 2^63 − 1], both under §3b's chain-order monotonicity invariants (GPT56-V90 F2/F3, CODEX-V90 F1: the pair was authenticated but unbounded, and signatures prove authorship, not monotonicity)** — and a bounded-decimal chain position; **never payload bytes, never a payload digest, never an outcome or refusal field** — value domains bound to BS-2k exactly as item (ii)'s are (GPT56-V88 F2, CODEX-V88 F1: the ruled second event class was defined at Row B, BELOW this exhaustive list, and so defaulted χ-bearing by the default rule — the list is extended AT the list, which is where exhaustiveness lives; the registry's `arrival.*` rows now cite THIS item as their source, not v9's slot schema, which never contained an arrival); 
(ii-c) **the CHECKPOINT CLOCK RECORD under its closed schema `(boot_epoch, monotonic_reading)` — and its OPENING form `(boot_epoch, monotonic_reading, predecessor_epoch, gap_declaration)`, the recovery checkpoint's extended record** — bounded decimals throughout (epochs in [0, 10^6] with 0 RESERVED as the first opening's predecessor — GPT56-V94 F2, CODEX-V94 F6: NONE had no encoding; readings in [0, 2^63 − 1] ns quantized to `g`, arrivals rounding DOWN and decisions/openings/checkpoints UP — GPT56-V94 F3, CODEX-V94 F3; the gap list ascending epochs), nothing else (GPT56-V93 F1, CODEX-V93 F1: the V93 predecessor/gap declaration had no schema to live in; the emptiness of a declared gap is proven by chain continuity, not trusted — spec §3b) — every checkpoint's materials carry one and the clock pass consumes them pre-unblinding (GPT56-V92 F2: consumed pre-unblinding while absent from this exhaustive list, χ-bearing by the default rule — the same defect as the arrival schema and the binding map before it, admitted AT the list like both); 
(ii-d) **the TERMINATED-family receipts' non-χ faces AND the SUCCESSOR EXPORT `(kind, sealed_enumeration_digest, continuation_segment_digest, terminal_head, freeze_signature_digest, flagged_keys)` (V109 — GPT56/CODEX-V108 F4; the sixth field joined at V111 and THIS item lagged a revision, still declaring the superseded five-field body — GPT56-V111 F3, the exact cross-declaration drift `ref/gen_nonchi_surface.py`'s field-echo now refuses mechanically; `flagged_keys` under its CANONICAL SET ENCODING — count-prefixed, lexicographically sorted canonical key bytes, duplicate-refusing, the empty set count 0, the sealed-set-digest precedent — CODEX-V111 F7: field presence without a unique encoding let one logical set own many byte strings) — and, for a run terminated BEFORE BS-L, the PRE-LOCK form `(kind, terminal_enumeration_digest, terminal_head, freeze_signature_digest, flagged_keys)` (GPT56-V112 F9: pre-lock termination had no constructible sealed_enumeration_digest, and an absent field must be absent BY FORM, never by sentinel; `terminal_enumeration_digest` the drain-cut enumeration set, its construction defined in the termination spec's export trigger row, and the two kinds are domain-separated, the verifier refusing a post-lock run emitting the pre-lock form and the converse)** — the EXHAUSTION HALT RECEIPT `(kind, chain_head, freeze_signature_digest, first_opening_digest)` under the interface's provisioned keypair, and the UNNAMEABLE-CLASS terminated-verdict record `(kind, class_key, gate, chain_head, freeze_signature_digest, first_opening_digest)` under the enumerator keypair — **faces synced with the live bodies (GPT56-V98 F6, CODEX-V98 F3: this exhaustive item lagged the run-binding fields one revision)** (GPT56-V96 F6: the second sibling's record was unspecified and unauthenticated while this list carried only the first); identities and everything else stay SEALED committee-side or in gate materials; 
(ii-e) **the GATE PASS RECORD `(gate, head_position, head_digest, verifier_digest, predecessor_record_digest, partition_cut_position, signature-enveloped)`** — `partition_cut_position` the issuance commit's last write position once BS-L exists, 0 before (GPT56-V97 F3) — **and the field is CORROBORATED, not self-attested (GPT56-V98 F3, CODEX-V98 F4: the verifier wrote the position it then checked): the issuance commit's writes are CHAIN EVENTS whose object identity is BS-L, they are CONTIGUOUS (one atomic commit by the single serialised writer), and BS-L's identity can never legitimately recur — §1b forbids mutation of a sealed artifact, so a second BS-L-writing episode is malformed history outright (GPT56-V99 F6, CODEX-V99 F3: events carry no transaction identity, so "last BS-L write" alone was not a commit boundary; contiguity plus unrepeatability make it one). The verifier RECOMPUTES the cut as the end of the SINGLE contiguous BS-L-writing run after the signed cut, refuses non-contiguity or recurrence, and refuses a record whose recorded cut differs; the chain, not the record, is the authority** — — the anchors themselves, load-bearing for every as-of-the-anchors claim and therefore ON the surface they defend (GPT56-V96 F1, CODEX-V96 F1); 
(ii-f) **the DRAIN-START record, the TERMINAL CHECKPOINT and the RECEIPT-NOTE — under §3c T1–T3's closed schemas, QUOTED LABEL-BOUND ABOVE AND NOT RESTATED HERE (GPT56-V105 F3, CODEX-V105 F3: this item's restated tuples drifted from the spec's within one revision — seven fields against eight — and name-set grouping is structurally blind to pure addition; a second copy is a second source, so the copy is gone and the T-quotes are the single surface)** — clock-bearing, under the clock pass, domain-tagged; 
(ii-g) **the VERIFICATION-READ record `(kind, request_key, touch_position, boot_epoch, monotonic_reading)`, the VERIFICATION-BOUNDARY record `(kind, gate, boot_epoch, monotonic_reading)`, the ATTEMPT-START record `(kind, member_position, boot_epoch, monotonic_reading)` the ATTEMPT-CLOSE record `(kind, member_position, close_class, boot_epoch, monotonic_reading)` and the VERIFICATION-CLOSE record `(kind, gate, boundary_position, close_class, boot_epoch, monotonic_reading)` (V114 — GPT56/CODEX-V113 F2: the retry cap needed a byte to count; `gate` the closed five-gate set as (ii-e)'s, `boundary_position` a bounded-decimal chain position, its qualified domain stated at the shared-fields sentence below) — the checkpoint-family record kinds V111 built and left OFF this list, χ-bearing by the default rule and so unlawful for the very consumers they were built for (GPT56-V111 F2 — the FOURTH integration lag of this exact shape, and the last hand-maintained one: the admission is now GENERATED-CHECKED, `ref/gen_nonchi_surface.py` refusing any registry record kind without an admission here, so a fifth instance cannot exist unnoticed): all appended by Row B under the chain's running-digest discipline, kinds domain-tagged, clock pairs bounded as (ii-c)'s and quantized UP, `ATTEMPT-CLOSE.close_class` the closed TWO-token set {`ABORTED`, `ABORTED-BY-RESTART`} and `VERIFICATION-CLOSE.close_class` the closed THREE-token set {`ABORTED`, `EXPIRED`, `ABORTED-BY-RESTART`} — each domain stated once, QUALIFIED BY ITS RECORD (GPT56-V114 F1, CODEX-V114 F5: an unqualified shared field name gave EXPIRED two incompatible domains in one exhaustive item, making the budget-expiry close simultaneously conforming and nonconforming), `touch_position` and `member_position` bounded-decimal chain positions, `request_key` as (ii-b)'s — never a payload byte, never an outcome beyond the closed close_class, never a χ-bearing field;** 
(iii) the producer checksum list (§2.5), exclusively for source images; 
(iv) fixtures and their transcripts, synthetic by construction; **(iv-b) the enumeration entries, their continuation segment, and their signed explanation artifacts (CODEX-V70 F3 — the pre-unblinding enumeration verifier must read these, and the closed-list default would have made them χ-bearing and unreadable): entries carry only positions, digests, closed tokens and signatures; explanations are bound by the same REBUILT principle as refusal reasons — storage state allowed, **nothing content-derived ever**, and an explanation describing measured properties is itself a violation to be refused; and the SIGNED REVIEW RECORD `(kind, reviewer_identity, review_timestamp, review_disposition, evidence_ref, reviewed_chain_position, reviewed_event_digest, reviewed_class_key, first_opening_digest)` — §6.1's review schema admitted AT the list, where exhaustiveness lives (GPT56-V111 F2): positions, digests, closed tokens and a roster-bound HUMAN signature — the disposition tokens *fault* and *tampering* value literals, never fields — and an emission-describing review is refused exactly as an explanation would be;** **(iv-c) the binding→key map — sealed segment and continuation entries alike — under its closed schema `(request_key, decision chain_position, decision event_digest, decision boot_epoch, decision monotonic_reading, signature)`: a bounded-decimal key, chain positions, digests, the decision's clock pair (same bounds as the arrival's, QUANTIZED to the BS-2k granularity `g` like every recorded reading — GPT56-V93 F7's channel bound — **WIDENED at V93 to carry the decide-within-D evidence, CODEX-V92 F1: the ruled access-log schema stays untouched and the map carries the clock instead; the widening is CONFIRMED — 2026-08-30 20:45 KST, the principal, verbatim "map widening confirmed as filed" (`MAP_WIDENING_CONFIRMATION_20260830.md`); reversal is a new ruling now, not a lapse (the filed-reversible-awaiting-confirmation posture ended with the ruling)**), and a provisioned-keypair signature, never a payload byte, never an outcome field (CODEX-V90 F2: the pre-opening verifier consumes this map, and an artifact off this exhaustive list is χ-bearing by default — the consumer was unlawful by the covenant's own rule until the map was DECLARED. This declares what the map already carried; it does not widen what it may carry, so the ruled refusal vocabulary is untouched. **The `signature` field is the 64-byte detached deterministic signature over the canonical entry body under Row B's provisioned keypair — no envelope leaves beyond the value; the signer is roster-bound by provisioning, not named per entry (CODEX-V91 F3: "sig-envelope" was an off-enum type inviting undeclared leaves).** **And the join's STRUCTURE is χ-blind by the FIFO rule (GPT56-V91 F7): decisions commit in arrival order, so the arrival→decision permutation is the identity; the residual — the POSITION GAP between an arrival and its decision, which varies with processing time under interleaved arrivals — is a NAMED RESIDUAL like the cause-token channel: bounded by the gates' review of the chain, and irremovable without removing the join itself**);** 
(v) the authenticated **acceptance-evidence projection** containing **only narrowly enumerated authenticated predicate bits** (e.g., `parent_attempt_present`, `byte_integrity_pass`, `canonical_shape_pass`), each mechanically recomputable by a separately pinned independent verifier. **All cutout digests stay inside the sealed boundary and are never exported.** No execution completion/non-finite status, no caller-authored status, and no free-form identifier may be exported. Every legal value is mechanically fixed. Attempt identity must be derived from a canonical source and serialization. Confidence is explicitly excluded from this projection and deferred to post-unblinding.

**THE STRING RULE IS AN ENUMERATED REGISTRY, NOT A UNIVERSAL SENTENCE (CODEX-V72 F2, GPT56-V72 F3 —
the V72 sentence was universal and the corpus already contained counterexamples: existing non-χ slot
receipts whose schemas constrain names but not value domains, and `explanation_ref` itself, which the
rule's own revision introduced unbounded. A universal claim written in one sitting is the anywhere/
never defect this register keeps cataloguing).** The rule survives as the DEFAULT, enumerated:
**`ref/STRING_FIELD_REGISTRY.md`, generated by `ref/gen_string_field_registry.py` from the schema
blocks themselves AND from frozen v9's `SLOT_SCHEMA`, its receipt ENVELOPE constructor, the enumerator signature, the non-slot artifact classes and **the environment's leaf fields — with the COUNT quoted from `ref/_registry_counts.txt` and from nowhere else: V77 still hand-said "nine" and "145" in this very sentence after declaring the counts generator-owned (GPT56-V77 F11, CODEX-V77 F5)** (V74's 123 omitted the envelope/environment fields v9 wraps every receipt in, the signature itself, and the non-slot classes — GPT56-V74 F1, CODEX-V74 F1; and the signature is constrained to a **deterministic scheme mandated at BS-2k**, because a valid ECDSA signature still carries a nonce — a covert channel wearing a validity proof). **The environment's six leaves are classified honestly — and at V77 the three value-unpinned ones (`python`, `platform`, `machine`) gained a REAL encoding bound, because "bounded-encoding" with no declared bound was a contradiction inside one registry row (GPT56-V76 F1, CODEX-V76 F2): **printable ASCII, ≤ 64 bytes, refused by the successor-layer envelope verifier** — unpinned in VALUE, since any conforming interpreter string passes, and bounded in ENCODING, which is what the column claims. Both halves are now true, and the residual surface (value freedom within the bound) stays named. And the non-slot rows stop being pseudo-fields (GPT56-V75 F2, CODEX-V75 F1): the inventoried classes are field-by-field in the registry itself and the rest are honest `SCHEMA-PENDING` stubs. **The COUNTS are the generator's, quoted from `ref/_registry_counts.txt`, never hand-written — V76 said "nine classes, six pending" and the generator then said ten and seven — a HISTORICAL value; the live counts are whatever `ref/_registry_counts.txt` says now, and GPT56-V81 F8 caught this sentence reading as current one revision after pending moved to eight (GPT56-V76 F3, CODEX-V76 F1), the hand-copied-count drift on the surface built while writing about that drift. The declared CANONICAL BODIES the extraction had missed — freeze-signature body, lock body, opening authorization, entry body, explanation body, provenance record — are registry rows now.** The registry records its generating draft and **the lint blocks when that is not the draft under lint** (CODEX-V75 F3): the first registry
enumerated only the draft-declared schemas and omitted all 86 fields of the EXISTING slot receipts
(GPT56-V73 F2, CODEX-V73 F1), the universal-sentence defect surviving into the enumeration built to
replace it. `BS-1b.provenance`, the free-prose field GPT56 named, is now a digest-ref. Stale
registry rows now EXIT NONZERO (CODEX-V73 F4): a stale row is extractor/schema drift — the signal
that caught the digit-blind bug — and a zero exit made that omission nonblocking** — the generator extracts field tokens from the ENUMERATED mechanically-parsed blocks and takes every other schema from HAND-DECLARED source sets — two provenances, named honestly per block (GPT56-V113 F4: the universal every-field claim was false — an unrecognized declaration shape was invisible to source, registry and surface together while every check stayed green; the generator now enumerates its parsed-vs-declared split, and the battery refuses any `closed schema (` declaration written outside the backticked grammar every extractor reads), so it
cannot silently omit a field the way a keyword filter omitted Row F; **a field with no registry row
is FORBIDDEN BY DEFAULT and the generator exits nonzero on it.** Each row carries the field's
constraint — `closed-vocab` · `bounded-encoding` · `digest-ref` — and where it is declared.
**`explanation_ref` is repaired to `digest-ref`: it is the sha256 of the canonical explanation body,
not an arbitrary identifier** (GPT56-V72 F3). **Value-domain enforcement for slot receipts is a
successor-layer obligation carried by `receipt_strict()` and the verifiers, since frozen v9 checks
names only** (CODEX-V72 F2); §11 carries the item. **And the honest limit, stated rather than
discovered:** bounded numerics still carry bits — a duration is a number — so the registry bounds
CAPACITY and cannot zero it; what it removes is free prose, which is unbounded capacity plus
deniability.

**Everything else is χ-bearing by default**: any artifact not on this list and not on the permitted surface; every per-object execution measurement receipt wherever it sits; the cutout-completion receipt; any opaque digest of χ-bearing bytes — a digest whose preimage's schema is not on this list or the permitted surface, the acceptance ledger's measurement digest among them, because such a digest is a verification oracle for a guessed outcome; the label-set receipt, which is χ-bearing and remains in the committee store; BS-7f, the post-unblinding adequacy receipt, and BS-V receipts; and any schema that permits outcome payloads. Doubt resolves toward χ-bearing.

**The sealed stores.** The *main sealed store* holds cutouts, instrument outputs, the cutout-completion receipt, and the acceptance ledger. The *committee sealed store* holds the hand-check labels and the label-set receipt. A third χ-bearing store — the predecessor archive — is governed by §6.2's seal-state rule and Row B's enforceable mediation. All three stores are provisioned at **BS-2k** (class-P DESIGN slot). Gates and referees are external witnesses: their inputs are the closed list of non-χ-bearing receipt classes and fixtures only, and no gate input is χ-bearing.

**The phase line.** P0 freeze → P1 BS-6, first image byte → P2 cutout production, pre-inference integrity projection, exact-parent C2 stage-completion, and instrument inference → P3 BS-2f → P4 BS-8f → P5 BS-5f → P6 BS-L, **the primary lock** → P7 unblinding → P8 BS-7f, post-unblinding adequacy receipt, and BS-V → P9 disclosure. 

**THE TABLE.** Each row is one permitted actor or process. Any pre-unblinding touch of a χ-bearing object by any person or process not in this table, or outside a row's stated surface, is forbidden by default.

Catalogue quality is carried only as an already-resolved pre-lock status that cannot constitute a P8 removal. **No discretionary retry.** This exact set-equality closure produces the canonical **post-unblinding adequacy receipt**, binding the parent-set digest, old BS-2f mask digest, complete terminal-state partition, final-mask digest, calibration applicability, the already-verified pre-unblinding calibration PASS, locked Stage-C PASS, protocol digest, and verifier result. Second, Row P binds the already-verified pre-unblinding calibration PASS (`a_LB_b >= 0.85`), relying on the locked BS-5f and BS-L verification rather than re-evaluating the threshold. These consequences are fixed before any real χ is read, citing §6.3 which voids any post-first-real-χ change to a decision threshold. | P8, after unblinding | the unblinding receipt and a verified BS-L | the **post-unblinding adequacy receipt**, then BS-7f, then BS-V | any execution before unblinding; any verdict produced outside this symbol; **silent inner-join loss; discretionary retry** |
